NPDev General Structure And Proposal
Generated at: 2026-04-22 23:29:15
Root: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General
IncludeFullDigests: False
ExpandMirroredSamples: False
ExpandEvidence: False

============================================================
HOW TO READ THIS FILE
============================================================

This is the clean handoff version of the NPDev_General structure and proposal.
It keeps the project-owned architecture, source files, schemas, templates, docs, scripts, samples, and PROJECT_DIGEST.md files.
It intentionally removes folders and files that are standard local tooling output or dependency cache.

The goal is to help a human or AI understand NPDev itself, not to study every downloaded React, Gradle, npm, IDE, or compiled artifact.

============================================================
AI READING ORDER
============================================================

1. D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
2. The target subproject PROJECT_DIGEST.md
3. Then the source tree of that subproject
4. Use D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevSamples only as examples/evidence, not as platform truth

============================================================
SENSITIVE / TRANSITIONAL AREAS
============================================================

- NPDevRuntimeHost still contains broad operational/runtime surfaces and should not be assumed to all be equally mature.
- Historical sample/domain words may still appear in resources/migrations and should not be treated as platform truth.
- Generated files are not canonical source.
- Samples are allowed to contain business vocabulary; platform core is not.

============================================================
WHAT WAS OMITTED AND WHY
============================================================

Omitted directories:
- .git
- .idea
- .gradle
- .vscode
- node_modules
- build
- dist
- out
- target
- coverage
- .vite
- .cache
- .next
- .turbo
- bin
- obj

Omitted file patterns:
- NPDev_ExtrucureAndProposal*.txt
- *.log
- *.tmp
- *.class
- *.jar
- *.war
- *.ear
- *.iml

Collapsed by default:
- Mirrored sample resource folders under NPDevContract, NPDevEditor, and NPDevGenerator. Expand with -ExpandMirroredSamples.
- Evidence/history folders such as run-output, migration-plans, schema-snapshots, db-history, and timestamped archives. Expand with -ExpandEvidence.
- Full PROJECT_DIGEST.md contents. Include with -IncludeFullDigests.

Meaning of the omissions:
- node_modules is npm dependency cache. It is recreated with npm ci and does not describe NPDev's architecture.
- .gradle, build, dist, out, target, bin, obj, coverage, and cache folders are generated outputs. They are recreated by build or test commands.
- .idea and .vscode are local IDE state. They can help one developer locally, but they should not define the platform.
- compiled packages such as .jar, .war, and .class files are delivery artifacts, not source-of-truth design.
- generated structure/proposal text files are omitted from the tree to avoid the report listing itself.

Important: source files are not omitted just because they are React, Java, Gradle, or PowerShell.
NPDevEditor source code, package.json, Vite configuration, tests, schemas, templates, scripts, and docs remain visible because they explain what NPDev owns.

============================================================
PROJECT DIGESTS
============================================================

Default mode includes short summaries only. Re-run with -IncludeFullDigests to inline the full digest contents.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDev_General
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Unites the split NPDev workspace and explains the product ambition, architecture philosophy, boundaries, risks, and reading path.
Receives: The complete NPDev_General folder containing the contract, runtime host, editor, generator, kernel, and samples.
Delivers: A shared mental model for humans and AI tools before editing any subproject.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\README.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev\script\restructure\03-export-npdev-general-structure-and-proposal.ps1
Main verification/build command: Documentation-only: verify the file exists and run this exporter to regenerate the handoff report.
Main risks/watch points:
- Adding root build files would accidentally couple projects that are intentionally independent.
- Treating samples or generated outputs as platform truth can reintroduce hardcoded business vocabulary.
- Ignoring subproject digests can cause edits in the wrong architectural layer.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevContract
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Defines the stable contracts shared across Editor, Generator, RuntimeHost, Kernel, and Samples.
Receives: Authoring models, config contracts, runtime manifest expectations, JSON schemas, and DSL fixture inputs.
Delivers: Schemas, contract documentation, examples, and the Java DSL artifact used by generator/runtime-facing code.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\schemas
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\dsl
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\docs
Main verification/build command: From D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevContract\dsl run: gradle test --no-daemon --console=plain
Main risks/watch points:
- Contract drift between schemas, Java DSL classes, and generator/runtime expectations.
- Putting platform behavior here instead of keeping contracts declarative.
- Letting sample-specific domain words become global contract vocabulary.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevRuntimeHost
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevRuntimeHost\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Hosts generated applications at execution time and wires operational runtime surfaces around generated artifacts.
Receives: Generated app artifacts, runtime manifests, kernel/adapters, database configuration, and operational scripts.
Delivers: A runnable host process/container for exercising generated systems and runtime behavior.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevRuntimeHost\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevRuntimeHost\BootstrapExec
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevRuntimeHost\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
Main verification/build command: Use the RuntimeHost digest for the current command; broad operational surfaces mean verification depends on the generated app scenario.
Main risks/watch points:
- Broad runtime/ops surfaces may not all be equally mature.
- Generated app behavior can be mistaken for host-owned platform behavior.
- Database/runtime scripts can contain transitional assumptions from older samples.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevEditor
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Provides the authoring UI for creating, editing, validating, loading, and exporting model/config inputs.
Receives: Contracts, schemas, user-authored model/config data, and sample fixtures for guided authoring.
Delivers: A React UI bundle/JAR that helps NPDev users produce validated handoff packages for generation.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor\ui-react
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor\build.gradle
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor\resources
Main verification/build command: From D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevEditor run: gradle clean build --no-daemon --console=plain
Main risks/watch points:
- UI can accidentally hardcode sample domains instead of reading model metadata.
- Authoring convenience can bypass schema/contract validation.
- npm dependency folders are local cache and must not be treated as source.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevGenerator
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Reads validated model/config inputs and generates application artifacts into a chosen output path.
Receives: Model/config JSON, schemas/contracts, templates, generation options, and sample inputs.
Delivers: Generated source/resources/manifests, generation reports, and migration/runtime artifacts for final apps.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator\generator
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator\templates
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator\tools
Main verification/build command: From D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevGenerator run: gradle :dsl:clean generatorQualityGate --no-daemon --console=plain
Main risks/watch points:
- Generated artifacts can be confused with canonical platform source.
- Template changes can leak sample-specific domain concepts into every generated app.
- Generator and kernel contract drift can break runtime behavior after generation.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevKernel
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Implements runtime semantics and capability ports used by generated applications during execution time.
Receives: Runtime manifests, generated app calls, adapters, request context, persistence/event/audit needs, and contract expectations.
Delivers: Kernel APIs, ports, adapters, runtime validation, flow execution, tracing, audit, auth, and persistence capabilities.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel\core
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel\kernel
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel\adapters
Main verification/build command: From D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevKernel run: gradle kernelQualityGate --no-daemon --console=plain
Main risks/watch points:
- Adapters can accidentally own business policy instead of implementing capability plumbing.
- Runtime validation/auth/audit behavior is high-risk and must remain model/manifest-driven.
- Do not run kernel and generator Gradle gates in parallel if they share sibling DSL build outputs.

------------------------------------------------------------
DIGEST SUMMARY: NPDevSamples
Full digest path: D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevSamples\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
------------------------------------------------------------

Purpose: Stores canonical examples, fixtures, and evidence used to test and explain NPDev without defining platform truth.
Receives: Representative business scenarios, model/config examples, scripts, and generated/test evidence.
Delivers: Canonical sample inputs and evidence that Editor, Generator, Contract, and RuntimeHost can reference.
Key entry points:
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevSamples\PROJECT_DIGEST.md
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevSamples\models
- D:\WorkSpace\NPDev_General\NPDevSamples\test-models
Main verification/build command: Use the scenario-specific scripts in NPDevSamples, then run the target Editor/Generator/RuntimeHost verification command.
Main risks/watch points:
- Samples may contain business vocabulary by design; that vocabulary must not become platform vocabulary.
- Evidence folders can be large and historical; inspect only when validating a scenario.
- Mirrored copies in other projects can lag behind the canonical sample source.
